- `hmh07-lua:046` (original lines 951-1001, 927-949): Creates a draft-loadout button plus preview and initializes the private-match exclusion model. Button activation branches on private/public/custom director state; custom recreates the datasource before invoking its preview helper. Nonzero XUID plus global preview flag controls preview states, with a separate primary-local-client branch. XUID changes recreate the list. The shared source contains no direct HMH gate; only its caller/state connection is recorded, and ZM helper internals remain conditional boundaries.

- `hmh07-lua:021` (original lines 951-1001, 927-949): Creates next/previous/random vote choices, binds MapVote names/images/counts and installs selection callbacks using their exact vote enums. Previous choice is unselectable when MapVotePreviousSelectable is false. State predicates distinguish voting, locked chosen next/previous/random and locked selected map. CallOriginalFirst setState may install a one-shot clip-over selected-state transition. The public director constructs this ZM-named widget unconditionally; this does not prove its ZM clip path or vote API is active for HMH. Preserve that membership boundary rather than excluding it by name.

- `hmh07-lua-follow1:010` (original lines 951-1001, 951-1001, 927-949, 927-949): Stopped Director constructs this child directly; its Arena name does not exclude construction. Builds map vote plus PC vote view, ready/blackmarket buttons, overhead names and arena event/progress views. MapVote model notifications update source state predicates; BlackMarket Hidden tests loot_enableBlackMarket while its action opens QuarterMaster and returns true. Ready model binds the exact cross button bits. IsPC state is a source predicate, not an inferred universal platform gate. Lobby client-data/character/lobbyNav callbacks update position-draft models only when ShowDirectorArenaMatchmaking(controller). Registers close, optional postload, initializes overhead names, returns self. The detailed conditional vote/callback contracts below do not prove arena gameplay or backend execution in HMH.

- `hmh07-lua-follow1:109` (original lines 1785-1803): RegisterEventHandlers returns outside demo playback; otherwise binds three demo events, sets up UI models and fetches fileshare content/marks dirty if readiness is0/false or user differs. AddHUDWidgets creates Demo only when playback, menu exists and no Demo; binds button model, initializes/focuses it and adds a close unsubscription wrapper. It adds reel/info widgets, prioritizes activateDemoStartScreen over openHighlightStartScreen, regenerates reel when moments>0 else shows error and pauses demo. PC pushes sticky elements. Outside demo it closes and nils existing Demo. These are conditional shared demo branches, not evidence live HMH executes them; request producers/services remain external.

- `hmh07-lua:186` (original lines 951-951, 951-1001, 927-927, 927-949): Hud_WZ directly constructs this result widget. SetupGameEndScoreWidget and DisableAutoButtonCallback run first; demo.processedKeyframeJump and lobbyRoot.publicLobby.stage are initialized to0 (real model writes, not backend stage proof). Builds result/outcome/eliminator/status/timer/deathcam/VOIP/quit/placement/killer/weapon/AAR/director/crosshair children. The constructor requests a GameScore/titleString subscription. Only if its callback is delivered and model:get() is nonnil, it requests LocalizeToUpperString with that value and passes the unknown result to Outcome:setText, if earlier accesses/calls succeed; nil takes no explicit setText arm. Callback delivery, localization/case result and displayed effect remain unresolved; aliveTeammateCount drives DownNotOut art; GameScore model forwarded to eliminator/challenge/killer/weapon displays. Victory controls challenge coin visibility. meansOfDeath>0 hides killer/weapon displays and inversely reveals self-caused/means-of-death displays. Attacker identity model rebinding first removes and nils old per-field subscription, dereferences new attackerClientModel, then subscribes xuid/clientNum/rank/rankIcon; analogous clientModel rebinding populates MistakesWereMade identity. Calling card receives full client model; nonnil callbacks update emblem/name/cleaned clan/rank/icon. No nil update clears old displayed text explicitly. VOIP HudStart has a recovered early-return/branch shape (exact trace) and ShowForCodCaster AlwaysFalse; do not replace it with an assumed visibility formula. Outcome state family: ShowOutcomeTransition with no killcam -> Victory/PC; defeat/team_elimination with no killcam -> Defeat/GameOver variants; elimination -> Elimination. Platoon variants additionally platoonCount!=0; PC variants additionally IsPC. Non-PC named states do not themselves contain not-IsPC, so do not assume exclusivity; LUI arbitration stays external. State/current/visibility/profile/lobby/teammate/AAR/input subscriptions reevaluate state/buttons. Square prioritizes console spectate (hide AAR/send skip_deathcam/clear transition/location/hide occluder), then console online AAR close under ui_showWZAAR/noncustom/not-spectate. Triangle redeploy requires private host, all members can redeploy, no matchmaking, redeploy_enabled and noncustom. Cross console deathcam requires eligibility, non-PC, not alternate killcam button, not UI-active/demo/default and viewed report; sends play_deathcam, sets hasPlayedDeathCam, plays alias/music. Circle console hold-finished quits; PC branches quit on non-held conditions; contextual2 handles PC exit. Each action returns true when taken, label callbacks often return false despite valid action, exact source retained. setState CallOriginalFirst hook branches demo/console/PC-occluded focus/intro/cursor/chat behavior; keyframeJump clears transition only during demo. Close is registered via CallOriginalSecond to an unselected __onClose; optional postload then focus/safe-area setup and return. Assets, backend result/rank/AAR provenance, hook order/cleanup outside scope, native menu response and actual execution remain open.

- `hmh07-lua:039` (original lines 951-1001, 927-949): Creates a10-item CalloutWheel, prompt/pointer and focus proxy. The exact recovered Visible predicate98–116 has no truthy normal return. If the initial IsCalloutsButtonPressed result is false/nil,115 returns that result. With a truthy initial result, a falsy EnableCalloutWheel result can return early at110; every other normally returning path through that initial truthy branch reaches the unconditional f7_local0=false at113 before115. Even when all five visibility exclusions and prematch/theater/laststand gates permit the inner assignment105 to compute true,113 overwrites it with false. Numeric0 is truthy and does not create another truthy return path. Helper/table/enum/provider errors may prevent any return; missing values do not establish feasibility of this predicate returning true. This is a preserved recovered-source control-flow limit, not a repair of the dump or proof of original game behavior. The registered setState callback, if delivered and its separate IsSelfInState(self,"Visible") test is truthy, requests MenuDelayedSetRightStickLock(menu,delivered controller,0,true); otherwise it requests that helper with125,false and then DisableWheelSelection. Those are requests, not established helper effects or timing. Length/degrees callbacks request selection changes only under their shown Visible guard; PC confirm requests a state reset. The callout and mouse-drag helper requests do not establish actual callback delivery, successful input locking, a server-visible callout event or native/VM/build/runtime behavior.

Finally create/reset forceScoreboard to0 regardless of PC. There are no subscription-handle/nil/field guards or cleanup/unsubscribe here; multiple calls can repeat registrations and reset models. Callback registration does not invoke callbacks. The f0_local3 value is captured by the registration call at that moment, while f0_local4 is read by its closure when delivered; original init order and native model/event delivery remain gaps. - `hmh07-lua-follow1:033` (original lines 498-505, 498-505, 951-1001, 951-1001): Creates Hud_MP_Codcaster menu, safe-area child and MP HUD components, current-weapon damage model binding, score notifications and spectator/profile state consumers. On score_event it prioritizes bounty score, then combat-efficiency score when spawn selection is inactive, then normal score. Objective ShowNotification predicates are AlwaysFalse here. HideAllButScoreboard is killcam/final/round/stage visibility union; SpeedBoost uses exact gadget id/state; HideForCodCaster requires spectator profile. X input is blocked only while AreXButtonPressesDisallowed; player_spawned outside draft disallows X400ms. Menu_loaded sizes PC to HUD area and console to safe area, dispatches to children when unhandled; then focus/state restoration, close registration, postload, back-button preservation, grenade warnings and cursor disable occur in literal order. Menu selection into this MP codcaster path remains conditional; no universal HMH requirement is inferred.

- `hmh07-lua:179` (original lines 951-951, 951-1001, 927-927, 927-949): Common lobby creates MapVotePC directly, alongside a separate MapVoteZM; a PC-looking field name is not a construction gate. Builds decided/random/previous/next entries. Model subscriptions update map images, names, mode names/icons and counts only for nonnil values; lobbyStatus text is uppercased. Confirm on random/next calls LobbyMapVoteSelectRandom/Next and returns true. Previous confirm and label require MapVotePreviousSelectable; disallowed confirm falls through without success and label returns false. Chosen child events play the corresponding parent clip then dispatch to children; focus handlers try local or super gainFocus, refresh confirm label and return its result. States are VOTING->MapVote, LOCKEDIN plus exact vote enum->ChosenNext/Previous/Random, and LOCKEDIN->SelectedMap. MapVote/lobbyNav/voteType notifications only schedule state reevaluation, not choose a vote. The setState CallOriginalFirst hook conditionally arranges SelectedMap on clip-over. Constructor returns self, close uses self.__onClose through CallOriginalSecond. Frozen class clip/reset/close companions are source-linked, with per-state transactions retained below; actual enum priority, backend vote delivery, callbacks, animation completion/cancellation and effective HMH visibility remain external.

- `hmh07-lua:231` (original lines 498-498, 498-505, 951-951, 951-1001, 927-927, 927-949): Supplied caller anchor is the Director factory definition itself, so external invocation/HMH menu navigation is unproven. Conditional constructor creates menu, setups default-focus callbacks/any-controller/offline-screen/build-info/companion-app/contracts model, then builds theater/custom/training/arena/public/private/pregame/select and shared/PC-looking children. Direct construction is not authority to reconstruct every other-mode child. Child menu_loaded wrappers use local/super menuLoaded, conditional non-PC safe-area sizing, then dispatch only if unhandled. State predicates: HideForGameStart from LobbyRoot hideMenusForGameStart1; ShowSelectKorea15Plus requires PC+Zombies+opaque Korea eligibility; other states delegate exact DirectorUtility ShowDirectorSelect/Pregame/Private/Custom/Arena/ArenaMatchmaking/Theater/Public/Training. lobbyNav/showSelect/hide flags reevaluate state; network/client/input/xuid/time/spinner/stage/session changes refresh button state. unused_gamepad_button invokes LobbyAddLocalClient(event.controller or owner) then dispatches children. Non-PC Start opens StartMenu_Main; non-PC social requires online/nonLAN/nonguest/allowed/not-demo-disabled/not-training, while PC select Triangle activates featured. Square branches offline-demo LAN hold behavior or nonPC select featured activation. Escape/mouse-keyboard and Circle/gamepad have separate ordered chains: hide preview, clear selected client, cancel/leave applicable custom/arena/theater/public states subject to spinner/timer/host/eligibility, or PC select quit. Duplicate preview branches are preserved and may be shadowed by earlier identical predicates; no guessed simplification. Back opens AAR only when CanShowAAR and refreshes prompt. RTrigger gestures requires dvar lobby_gestures_enabled plus opaque hash_61E20A632CB63092; then alias/preserve-button/open selector. setState CallOriginalFirst hook conditionally opens AAR/refreshes contract/client updates or initializes training; external predicates keep non-HMH internals conditional. Close CallOriginalFirst hook clears saved state and OnDirectorClose; class __onClose registered via CallOriginalSecond outside selected body remains dependency. lobby room/isMatchmaking callbacks refresh room; hideMenusForGameStart1 plays hide clip+loses focus/hides cursor,0 plays default/unhides cursor. menu_loaded then optional restore/postload; PC branch additionally key-color/friends/Korea/navigation/ZM-availability hooks, console branch DirectorUtility.PostLoad only. Exact default-focus registrations occur at end, then self returned. No live lobby/account action was executed; backend/model/enum/native dispatch, actual visibility and clip/close bodies remain open.

- `hmh07-main2:001` (original lines 1785-1803, 1785-1803): Read controller from event argument1; obtain VehicleInfo model then vehicleType:get() without handles/field guards. If that value is truthy, request already-saved f0_local11(menu,controller,type,true), explicitly forcing its vehicle HUD path. Next SafeGetModelValue(CurrentWeapon.getModel(controller),weaponReticle); if truthy request saved f0_local13(menu,controller,reticle,true). Then call f0_local4(menu,event), then f0_local2(menu,event), in that order unconditionally. Nil/false skip only the respective replacement request; Lua numeric0 remains truthy. No return, model write, error rollback, callback dispatch or resource cleanup in this wrapper; helper effects are separate.

- `hmh07-lua-follow4:099` (original lines 1606-1606, 1684-1684, 1606-1606, 1684-1684, 1606-1606, 1684-1684): Hides the vertical scrollbar when totalYDistance is zero. Otherwise shows it and computes the displayed fraction from (viewHeight - totalYDistance) / viewHeight; top-anchored and bottom-anchored branches set different top/bottom percentages from currentYOffset. No zero-height guard is shown. Uses 500/50 scroll-repeat values and defaults an absent target to the root. Interactive mode enables up/down mouse buttons and registers bar press/release/lose-focus/capture-lost/close/drag handlers plus background paging. Press asserts unlocked focus, captures the mouse and saves contact offsets. Release/lose-focus/close release capture and clear state; capture-lost clears state. Drag clamps normalized Y, optionally reverses it and scrolls. The disabled branch keeps bar/background unfocusable on updates. Wheel-down/up callbacks still register after either branch, scroll by opposite wheelScrollUnit values and return true; their prompt callbacks return false. When ContextualMenu exists, removes matching focusable paths during an ipairs traversal, closes and clears the contextual menu, then attaches a one-unit timer that force-notifies FreeCursor.position. In-place removals have the literal Lua traversal behavior; no compensation is invented.

- `hmh07-lua:184` (original lines 498-498, 498-505, 951-951, 951-1001, 927-927, 927-949): ui/main.lua1132..1165 builds common Hud unless existing/excluded gametype, then chooses WZ suffix for CoD.isWarzone OR gametype warzone (Campaign and CODFU/DOA overrides retained), and only when no T7HudMenuGameMode invokes Hud_WZ through dynamic factory unless non-WZ codcaster special case applies. Original effective flags/build remain conditional. Constructor initializes aarVisible/aarViewed only if unset, disabling automatic buttons only on PC, creates safe-area/ready/damage/chat/objective/results/scoreboard/hint/wheel/PC widgets. ReadyEvents scriptNotify dispatches hero_weapon_received/killstreak_received/levelup helpers; objective ShowNotification defaults are AlwaysFalse. MPHintText nonempty predicate and nonnil text link make this shared MP-named child usable on WZ. Sprays Visible requires held button plus no prematch-hide/last-stand/inventory/multi-pickup/remote/vehicle/demo/dead/round-end/scoreboard/spectator exclusions; Callouts similarly requires pressed+enabled and no theater/prematch/inventory/last-stand/dead/round-end/scoreboard/spectator exclusion. All corresponding model/input links reevaluate state, never produce gameplay input by themselves. Self states: HideAllButScoreboard=stage_ended; SpeedBoost=gadget_speed_burst plus gadget2 inuse; HideForCodCaster=caster with profile playerhud1. Circle/back hides scoreboard only through predicates and blocks the game event. Up opens quick inventory only with HUD weapon visibility, allowed inventory, no ended/demo/dead/scoreboard/spectating/UI/laststand/vehicle-hide/disable-setting; Down closes with its separate narrower gate. Weapon-next/prev/primary/secondary require Warzone, full inventory open and no demo. LB/RB/triggers and Cross prioritize health/equipment cycle actions when quick inventory open/profile cycle-items1/button-mapping agrees; they block the game event. Cross then has PC-gamepad deathcam fallback. PC-only spectate/deathcam/AAR/report branches retain exact additional conditions; if IsPC=false they do not execute, without excluding unconditional PC-looking child construction. CanUseQuickInventory notification closes quick inventory only if now disallowed and already open; character_unlock_update dispatches UpdateUnlockQuestState. menu_loaded processed, linked __onClose hook registered, captured f0_local0 postload invoked if present. PC final setup adds region checks/sizing; console final setup disables keyboard navigation and adds WZ armblade reticles; both setup grenade warnings/custom focus/cursor rules. Return self. Full exact callbacks/labels/branch ordering are in trace; unselected class clips/reset/close and external helper bodies are dependencies only, with no claim of original event delivery, network menu response or real execution.
